# Interne plannen (auto_explain, rol authenticated, JWT 0,9 kB). De RLS-boilerplate
# (InitPlans voor auth.uid()/profielen, 'Result'-nodes, 'One-Time Filter') is weggelaten;
# de gehashte SubPlans op documenten (de policies) staan er nog in.

duration: 94.458 ms  plan:
Query Text: 
  select
    c.id,
    c.document_id,
    c.tekst,
    c.pagina,
    c.paragraaf,
    c.chunk_index,
    d.titel,
    d.bron,
    d.bibliotheek,
    d.opslag_pad,
    ts_rank_cd(c.zoek_vector, q.query) as rang,
    c.documentstatus,
    c.bronstatus,
    c.documentdatum,
    c.geldig_vanaf,
    c.geldig_tot,
    c.procesinstantie_id,
    c.bronorganisatie,
    c.normgewicht,
    c.extern_url,
    d.fonds_id,
    d.volgende_review,
    c.wettelijk_regime
  from public.document_chunks c
  join public.documenten d on d.id = c.document_id
  cross join websearch_to_tsquery('dutch', p_query) as q(query)
  where d.actief = true
    -- 0154 §3: gearchiveerd universeel uit (NULL-veilig).
    and c.documentstatus is distinct from 'gearchiveerd'
    and c.zoek_vector @@ q.query
    and (p_document_ids is null or c.document_id = any(p_document_ids))
    and (
      p_modus is distinct from 'actueel'
      or (
        c.documentstatus in ('vastgesteld','van_kracht')
        and coalesce(c.bronstatus,'actief') = 'actief'
        and (c.geldig_vanaf is null or c.geldig_vanaf <= p_peildatum)
        and (c.geldig_tot   is null or c.geldig_tot   >= p_peildatum)
      )
    )
    and (p_bronstatus          is null or coalesce(c.bronstatus,'actief') = any(p_bronstatus))
    and (p_documentstatus      is null or c.documentstatus     = any(p_documentstatus))
    and (p_procesinstantie_ids is null or c.procesinstantie_id = any(p_procesinstantie_ids))
    and (p_bronsoort           is null or c.bibliotheek         = any(p_bronsoort))
    and (p_fonds_id is null or d.fonds_id = p_fonds_id or c.bibliotheek = 'generiek')
    and (
      c.bibliotheek is distinct from 'generiek'
      or (
        c.documentstatus = 'van_kracht'
        and coalesce(c.bronstatus,'actief') = 'actief'
        and (d.volgende_review is null or d.volgende_review >= p_peildatum)
      )
    )
  order by rang desc, c.chunk_index asc
  limit greatest(p_limit, 1);

    c.wettelijk_regime

  where d.actief = true

      )

  order by rang desc, c.chunk_index asc
  limit greatest(p_limit, 1);

duration: 510.611 ms  plan:
Query Text: 
  with q as (
    select websearch_to_tsquery('dutch', p_query) as tsq
  ),
  fts as (
    select dc.id,
           row_number() over (order by ts_rank_cd(dc.zoek_vector, q.tsq) desc, dc.id) as r
    from public.document_chunks dc
    join public.documenten d on d.id = dc.document_id
    cross join q
    where d.actief = true
      and dc.documentstatus is distinct from 'gearchiveerd'   -- 0154 §3 (NULL-veilig)
      and dc.zoek_vector @@ q.tsq
      and (p_document_ids is null or dc.document_id = any(p_document_ids))
      and (
        p_modus is distinct from 'actueel'
        or (
          dc.documentstatus in ('vastgesteld','van_kracht')
          and coalesce(dc.bronstatus,'actief') = 'actief'
          and (dc.geldig_vanaf is null or dc.geldig_vanaf <= p_peildatum)
          and (dc.geldig_tot   is null or dc.geldig_tot   >= p_peildatum)
        )
      )
      and (p_bronstatus          is null or coalesce(dc.bronstatus,'actief') = any(p_bronstatus))
      and (p_documentstatus      is null or dc.documentstatus     = any(p_documentstatus))
      and (p_procesinstantie_ids is null or dc.procesinstantie_id = any(p_procesinstantie_ids))
      and (p_bronsoort           is null or dc.bibliotheek         = any(p_bronsoort))
      and (p_fonds_id is null or d.fonds_id = p_fonds_id or dc.bibliotheek = 'generiek')
      and (
        dc.bibliotheek is distinct from 'generiek'
        or (
          dc.documentstatus = 'van_kracht'
          and coalesce(dc.bronstatus,'actief') = 'actief'
          and (d.volgende_review is null or d.volgende_review >= p_peildatum)
        )
      )
    order by ts_rank_cd(dc.zoek_vector, q.tsq) desc, dc.id
    limit p_kandidaten
  ),
  vec as (
    select dc.id,
           row_number() over (order by dc.embedding <=> p_embedding, dc.id) as r
    from public.document_chunks dc
    join public.documenten d on d.id = dc.document_id
    where d.actief = true
      and dc.documentstatus is distinct from 'gearchiveerd'   -- 0154 §3 (NULL-veilig)
      and dc.embedding is not null
      and (p_document_ids is null or dc.document_id = any(p_document_ids))
      and (
        p_modus is distinct from 'actueel'
        or (
          dc.documentstatus in ('vastgesteld','van_kracht')
          and coalesce(dc.bronstatus,'actief') = 'actief'
          and (dc.geldig_vanaf is null or dc.geldig_vanaf <= p_peildatum)
          and (dc.geldig_tot   is null or dc.geldig_tot   >= p_peildatum)
        )
      )
      and (p_bronstatus          is null or coalesce(dc.bronstatus,'actief') = any(p_bronstatus))
      and (p_documentstatus      is null or dc.documentstatus     = any(p_documentstatus))
      and (p_procesinstantie_ids is null or dc.procesinstantie_id = any(p_procesinstantie_ids))
      and (p_bronsoort           is null or dc.bibliotheek         = any(p_bronsoort))
      and (p_fonds_id is null or d.fonds_id = p_fonds_id or dc.bibliotheek = 'generiek')
      and (
        dc.bibliotheek is distinct from 'generiek'
        or (
          dc.documentstatus = 'van_kracht'
          and coalesce(dc.bronstatus,'actief') = 'actief'
          and (d.volgende_review is null or d.volgende_review >= p_peildatum)
        )
      )
    order by dc.embedding <=> p_embedding, dc.id
    limit p_kandidaten
  ),
  samen as (
    select coalesce(fts.id, vec.id) as id,
           fts.r as fts_rang,
           vec.r as vec_rang,
           coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + fts.r), 0) + coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + vec.r), 0) as rrf
    from fts
    full outer join vec on fts.id = vec.id
  )
  select dc.id, dc.document_id, dc.tekst, dc.pagina, dc.paragraaf, dc.chunk_index,
         d.titel, d.bron, d.bibliotheek, d.opslag_pad,
         s.rrf::real as rang, s.fts_rang, s.vec_rang,
         dc.documentstatus, dc.bronstatus, dc.documentdatum,
         dc.geldig_vanaf, dc.geldig_tot, dc.procesinstantie_id,
         dc.bronorganisatie, dc.normgewicht, dc.extern_url,
         d.fonds_id,
         d.volgende_review,
         dc.wettelijk_regime
  from samen s
  join public.document_chunks dc on dc.id = s.id
  join public.documenten d on d.id = dc.document_id
  where d.actief = true
  order by s.rrf desc, dc.id
  limit p_limit;

duration: 658.071 ms  plan:
Query Text: 
  with q as (
    select websearch_to_tsquery('dutch', p_query) as tsq
  ),
  fts as (
    select dc.id,
           row_number() over (order by ts_rank_cd(dc.zoek_vector, q.tsq) desc, dc.id) as r
    from public.document_chunks dc
    join public.documenten d on d.id = dc.document_id
    cross join q
    where d.actief = true
      and dc.documentstatus is distinct from 'gearchiveerd'   -- 0154 §3 (NULL-veilig)
      and dc.zoek_vector @@ q.tsq
      and (p_document_ids is null or dc.document_id = any(p_document_ids))
      and (
        p_modus is distinct from 'actueel'
        or (
          dc.documentstatus in ('vastgesteld','van_kracht')
          and coalesce(dc.bronstatus,'actief') = 'actief'
          and (dc.geldig_vanaf is null or dc.geldig_vanaf <= p_peildatum)
          and (dc.geldig_tot   is null or dc.geldig_tot   >= p_peildatum)
        )
      )
      and (p_bronstatus          is null or coalesce(dc.bronstatus,'actief') = any(p_bronstatus))
      and (p_documentstatus      is null or dc.documentstatus     = any(p_documentstatus))
      and (p_procesinstantie_ids is null or dc.procesinstantie_id = any(p_procesinstantie_ids))
      and (p_bronsoort           is null or dc.bibliotheek         = any(p_bronsoort))
      and (p_fonds_id is null or d.fonds_id = p_fonds_id or dc.bibliotheek = 'generiek')
      and (
        dc.bibliotheek is distinct from 'generiek'
        or (
          dc.documentstatus = 'van_kracht'
          and coalesce(dc.bronstatus,'actief') = 'actief'
          and (d.volgende_review is null or d.volgende_review >= p_peildatum)
        )
      )
    order by ts_rank_cd(dc.zoek_vector, q.tsq) desc, dc.id
    limit p_kandidaten
  ),
  vec as (
    select dc.id,
           row_number() over (order by dc.embedding <=> p_embedding, dc.id) as r
    from public.document_chunks dc
    join public.documenten d on d.id = dc.document_id
    where d.actief = true
      and dc.documentstatus is distinct from 'gearchiveerd'   -- 0154 §3 (NULL-veilig)
      and dc.embedding is not null
      and (p_document_ids is null or dc.document_id = any(p_document_ids))
      and (
        p_modus is distinct from 'actueel'
        or (
          dc.documentstatus in ('vastgesteld','van_kracht')
          and coalesce(dc.bronstatus,'actief') = 'actief'
          and (dc.geldig_vanaf is null or dc.geldig_vanaf <= p_peildatum)
          and (dc.geldig_tot   is null or dc.geldig_tot   >= p_peildatum)
        )
      )
      and (p_bronstatus          is null or coalesce(dc.bronstatus,'actief') = any(p_bronstatus))
      and (p_documentstatus      is null or dc.documentstatus     = any(p_documentstatus))
      and (p_procesinstantie_ids is null or dc.procesinstantie_id = any(p_procesinstantie_ids))
      and (p_bronsoort           is null or dc.bibliotheek         = any(p_bronsoort))
      and (p_fonds_id is null or d.fonds_id = p_fonds_id or dc.bibliotheek = 'generiek')
      and (
        dc.bibliotheek is distinct from 'generiek'
        or (
          dc.documentstatus = 'van_kracht'
          and coalesce(dc.bronstatus,'actief') = 'actief'
          and (d.volgende_review is null or d.volgende_review >= p_peildatum)
        )
      )
    order by dc.embedding <=> p_embedding, dc.id
    limit p_kandidaten
  ),
  samen as (
    select coalesce(fts.id, vec.id) as id,
           fts.r as fts_rang,
           vec.r as vec_rang,
           coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + fts.r), 0) + coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + vec.r), 0) as rrf
    from fts
    full outer join vec on fts.id = vec.id
  )
  select dc.id, dc.document_id, dc.tekst, dc.pagina, dc.paragraaf, dc.chunk_index,
         d.titel, d.bron, d.bibliotheek, d.opslag_pad,
         s.rrf::real as rang, s.fts_rang, s.vec_rang,
         dc.documentstatus, dc.bronstatus, dc.documentdatum,
         dc.geldig_vanaf, dc.geldig_tot, dc.procesinstantie_id,
         dc.bronorganisatie, dc.normgewicht, dc.extern_url,
         d.fonds_id,
         d.volgende_review,
         dc.wettelijk_regime
  from samen s
  join public.document_chunks dc on dc.id = s.id
  join public.documenten d on d.id = dc.document_id
  where d.actief = true
  order by s.rrf desc, dc.id
  limit p_limit;

  select d.id
    from public.documenten d
   where (p_lek = 'actief' or d.actief = true)

     and (p_lek = 'bronsoort' or p_bronsoort is null or d.bibliotheek = any(p_bronsoort))

          or (d.status = 'van_kracht' and coalesce(d.bronstatus, 'actief') = 'actief'
              and (p_lek = 'review' or d.volgende_review is null or d.volgende_review >= p_peildatum)))
     and (p_lek = 'actueel' or p_modus is distinct from 'actueel'
          or (d.status in ('vastgesteld', 'van_kracht')
              and (p_lek = 'bronstatus' or coalesce(d.bronstatus, 'actief') = 'actief')
              and (d.geldig_vanaf is null or d.geldig_vanaf <= p_peildatum)

              and d.scan_resultaat ->> 'verdict' = 'clean'
              and d.scan_resultaat ->> 'sha256' = d.bestand_hash))

duration: 14.683 ms  plan:
Query Text: 
  -- Vorm: id-begrensd via `document_id = any(array(...))` ⇒ bitmap-indexscan
  -- op idx_chunks_document (O(zichtbare chunks)); de join-vorm (docs join
  -- chunks) kreeg een seq scan over álle chunks met `@@` als filter. `@@` is
  -- niet leakproof en kan onder RLS nooit via de GIN lopen; hij blijft filter.
  -- `cross join websearch_to_tsquery(...)`: in het generieke plan van een
  -- SQL-functie wordt een inline `websearch_to_tsquery('dutch', $1)` anders per
  -- rij opnieuw geparset (gemeten: 70 ms i.p.v. 9 ms bij gelijke buffers).
  select c.id, c.document_id,
         ts_rank_cd(c.zoek_vector, q.tsq) as rang
    from public.document_chunks c
   cross join websearch_to_tsquery('dutch', p_query) as q(tsq)
   where c.document_id = any (array(select pr0_proto.toelaatbare_documenten(p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_fonds_id, p_scan, p_lek)))
     and c.zoek_vector @@ q.tsq
     and (p_lek = 'gearchiveerd' or c.documentstatus is distinct from 'gearchiveerd')
   order by rang desc, c.chunk_index asc, c.id
   limit greatest(p_limit, 1)

duration: 7.948 ms  plan:
Query Text: 
  -- Vorm: id-begrensd via `document_id = any(array(...))` ⇒ bitmap-indexscan
  -- op idx_chunks_document (O(zichtbare chunks)); de join-vorm (docs join
  -- chunks) kreeg een seq scan over álle chunks met `@@` als filter. `@@` is
  -- niet leakproof en kan onder RLS nooit via de GIN lopen; hij blijft filter.
  -- `cross join websearch_to_tsquery(...)`: in het generieke plan van een
  -- SQL-functie wordt een inline `websearch_to_tsquery('dutch', $1)` anders per
  -- rij opnieuw geparset (gemeten: 70 ms i.p.v. 9 ms bij gelijke buffers).
  select c.id, c.document_id,
         ts_rank_cd(c.zoek_vector, q.tsq) as rang
    from public.document_chunks c
   cross join websearch_to_tsquery('dutch', p_query) as q(tsq)
   where c.document_id = any (array(select pr0_proto.toelaatbare_documenten(p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_fonds_id, p_scan, p_lek)))
     and c.zoek_vector @@ q.tsq
     and (p_lek = 'gearchiveerd' or c.documentstatus is distinct from 'gearchiveerd')
   order by rang desc, c.chunk_index asc, c.id
   limit greatest(p_limit, 1)

Limit  (cost=5260.53..5260.54 rows=1 width=40) (actual rows=0 loops=1)

        Sort Method: quicksort  Memory: 25kB
        Buffers: shared hit=5391

              Rows Removed by Join Filter: 19111
              Buffers: shared hit=5391
              ->  Function Scan on websearch_to_tsquery q  (cost=0.25..0.26 rows=1 width=32) (actual rows=1 loops=1)
              ->  Bitmap Heap Scan on document_chunks c  (cost=80.92..5236.91 rows=1792 width=951) (actual rows=19111 loops=1)
                    Recheck Cond: (document_id = ANY ((InitPlan 1).col1))
                    Filter: (((ANY (document_id = (hashed SubPlan 7).col1)) OR (((InitPlan 8).col1 IS NOT NULL) AND (ANY (document_id = (hashed SubPlan 14).col1)))) AND (($9 = 'gearchiveerd'::text) OR (documentstatus IS DISTINCT FROM 'gearchiveerd'::text)))
                    Heap Blocks: exact=4763

duration: 8.487 ms  plan:
Query Text: 
  -- Vorm: id-begrensd via `document_id = any(array(...))` ⇒ bitmap-indexscan
  -- op idx_chunks_document (O(zichtbare chunks)); de join-vorm (docs join
  -- chunks) kreeg een seq scan over álle chunks met `@@` als filter. `@@` is
  -- niet leakproof en kan onder RLS nooit via de GIN lopen; hij blijft filter.
  -- `cross join websearch_to_tsquery(...)`: in het generieke plan van een
  -- SQL-functie wordt een inline `websearch_to_tsquery('dutch', $1)` anders per
  -- rij opnieuw geparset (gemeten: 70 ms i.p.v. 9 ms bij gelijke buffers).
  select c.id, c.document_id,
         ts_rank_cd(c.zoek_vector, q.tsq) as rang
    from public.document_chunks c
   cross join websearch_to_tsquery('dutch', p_query) as q(tsq)
   where c.document_id = any (array(select pr0_proto.toelaatbare_documenten(p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_fonds_id, p_scan, p_lek)))
     and c.zoek_vector @@ q.tsq
     and (p_lek = 'gearchiveerd' or c.documentstatus is distinct from 'gearchiveerd')
   order by rang desc, c.chunk_index asc, c.id
   limit greatest(p_limit, 1)

Query Text: 
  with fts as (
    select f.id, f.document_id, row_number() over (order by f.rang desc, f.id) as r
      from pr0_proto.r1_fts(p_query, p_kandidaten, p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_fonds_id, p_scan, p_lek) f
  ),
  vec as (
    select v.id, v.document_id, row_number() over (order by v.afstand, v.id) as r
      from pr0_proto.r1_vec(p_embedding, p_kandidaten, p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_fonds_id, p_scan, p_lek, p_iteratief, p_strategie) v
  )
  select coalesce(fts.id, vec.id), coalesce(fts.document_id, vec.document_id),
         (coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + fts.r), 0) + coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + vec.r), 0))::real as rang,
         fts.r::int, vec.r::int
    from fts full outer join vec on fts.id = vec.id
   order by 3 desc, 1
   limit p_limit

Query Text: 
  with fts as (
    select f.id, f.document_id, row_number() over (order by f.rang desc, f.id) as r
      from pr0_proto.r1_fts(p_query, p_kandidaten, p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_fonds_id, p_scan, p_lek) f
  ),
  vec as (
    select v.id, v.document_id, row_number() over (order by v.afstand, v.id) as r
      from pr0_proto.r1_vec(p_embedding, p_kandidaten, p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_fonds_id, p_scan, p_lek, p_iteratief, p_strategie) v
  )
  select coalesce(fts.id, vec.id), coalesce(fts.document_id, vec.document_id),
         (coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + fts.r), 0) + coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + vec.r), 0))::real as rang,
         fts.r::int, vec.r::int
    from fts full outer join vec on fts.id = vec.id
   order by 3 desc, 1
   limit p_limit

duration: 8.771 ms  plan:
Query Text: 
  -- Vorm: id-begrensd via `document_id = any(array(...))` ⇒ bitmap-indexscan
  -- op idx_chunks_document (O(zichtbare chunks)); de join-vorm (docs join
  -- chunks) kreeg een seq scan over álle chunks met `@@` als filter. `@@` is
  -- niet leakproof en kan onder RLS nooit via de GIN lopen; hij blijft filter.
  -- `cross join websearch_to_tsquery(...)`: in het generieke plan van een
  -- SQL-functie wordt een inline `websearch_to_tsquery('dutch', $1)` anders per
  -- rij opnieuw geparset (gemeten: 70 ms i.p.v. 9 ms bij gelijke buffers).
  select c.id, c.document_id,
         ts_rank_cd(c.zoek_vector, q.tsq) as rang
    from public.document_chunks c
   cross join websearch_to_tsquery('dutch', p_query) as q(tsq)
   where c.document_id = any (array(select pr0_proto.toelaatbare_documenten(p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_fonds_id, p_scan, p_lek)))
     and c.zoek_vector @@ q.tsq
     and (p_lek = 'gearchiveerd' or c.documentstatus is distinct from 'gearchiveerd')
   order by rang desc, c.chunk_index asc, c.id
   limit greatest(p_limit, 1)

Query Text: 
  with fts as (
    select f.id, f.document_id, row_number() over (order by f.rang desc, f.id) as r
      from pr0_proto.r1_fts(p_query, p_kandidaten, p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_fonds_id, p_scan, p_lek) f
  ),
  vec as (
    select v.id, v.document_id, row_number() over (order by v.afstand, v.id) as r
      from pr0_proto.r1_vec(p_embedding, p_kandidaten, p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_fonds_id, p_scan, p_lek, p_iteratief, p_strategie) v
  )
  select coalesce(fts.id, vec.id), coalesce(fts.document_id, vec.document_id),
         (coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + fts.r), 0) + coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + vec.r), 0))::real as rang,
         fts.r::int, vec.r::int
    from fts full outer join vec on fts.id = vec.id
   order by 3 desc, 1
   limit p_limit

duration: 9.109 ms  plan:
Query Text: 
  -- Vorm: id-begrensd via `document_id = any(array(...))` ⇒ bitmap-indexscan
  -- op idx_chunks_document (O(zichtbare chunks)); de join-vorm (docs join
  -- chunks) kreeg een seq scan over álle chunks met `@@` als filter. `@@` is
  -- niet leakproof en kan onder RLS nooit via de GIN lopen; hij blijft filter.
  -- `cross join websearch_to_tsquery(...)`: in het generieke plan van een
  -- SQL-functie wordt een inline `websearch_to_tsquery('dutch', $1)` anders per
  -- rij opnieuw geparset (gemeten: 70 ms i.p.v. 9 ms bij gelijke buffers).
  select c.id, c.document_id,
         ts_rank_cd(c.zoek_vector, q.tsq) as rang
    from public.document_chunks c
   cross join websearch_to_tsquery('dutch', p_query) as q(tsq)
   where c.document_id = any (array(select pr0_proto.toelaatbare_documenten(p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_fonds_id, p_scan, p_lek)))
     and c.zoek_vector @@ q.tsq
     and (p_lek = 'gearchiveerd' or c.documentstatus is distinct from 'gearchiveerd')
   order by rang desc, c.chunk_index asc, c.id
   limit greatest(p_limit, 1)

Query Text: 
  with fts as (
    select f.id, f.document_id, row_number() over (order by f.rang desc, f.id) as r
      from pr0_proto.r1_fts(p_query, p_kandidaten, p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_fonds_id, p_scan, p_lek) f
  ),
  vec as (
    select v.id, v.document_id, row_number() over (order by v.afstand, v.id) as r
      from pr0_proto.r1_vec(p_embedding, p_kandidaten, p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_fonds_id, p_scan, p_lek, p_iteratief, p_strategie) v
  )
  select coalesce(fts.id, vec.id), coalesce(fts.document_id, vec.document_id),
         (coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + fts.r), 0) + coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + vec.r), 0))::real as rang,
         fts.r::int, vec.r::int
    from fts full outer join vec on fts.id = vec.id
   order by 3 desc, 1
   limit p_limit

  select d.id
    from public.documenten d
   where (p_lek = 'actief' or d.actief = true)
     and (p_lek = 'gearchiveerd' or d.status is distinct from 'gearchiveerd')
     and (p_lek = 'scope' or p_document_ids is null or d.id = any(p_document_ids))
     and (p_lek = 'fonds' or p_fonds_id is null or d.fonds_id = p_fonds_id or d.bibliotheek = 'generiek')
     and (p_lek = 'bronsoort' or p_bronsoort is null or d.bibliotheek = any(p_bronsoort))
     and (p_lek = 'generiek_published' or d.bibliotheek is distinct from 'generiek'
          or (d.status = 'van_kracht' and coalesce(d.bronstatus, 'actief') = 'actief'
              and (p_lek = 'review' or d.volgende_review is null or d.volgende_review >= p_peildatum)))
     and (p_lek = 'actueel' or p_modus is distinct from 'actueel'

              and (p_lek = 'review' or d.volgende_review is null or d.volgende_review >= p_peildatum)))

          or (d.bestand_hash ~ '^[a-f0-9]{64}$'

duration: 8.141 ms  plan:
Query Text: 
  -- Vorm: id-begrensd via `document_id = any(array(...))` ⇒ bitmap-indexscan
  -- op idx_chunks_document (O(zichtbare chunks)); de join-vorm (docs join
  -- chunks) kreeg een seq scan over álle chunks met `@@` als filter. `@@` is
  -- niet leakproof en kan onder RLS nooit via de GIN lopen; hij blijft filter.
  -- `cross join websearch_to_tsquery(...)`: in het generieke plan van een
  -- SQL-functie wordt een inline `websearch_to_tsquery('dutch', $1)` anders per
  -- rij opnieuw geparset (gemeten: 70 ms i.p.v. 9 ms bij gelijke buffers).
  select c.id, c.document_id,
         ts_rank_cd(c.zoek_vector, q.tsq) as rang
    from public.document_chunks c
   cross join websearch_to_tsquery('dutch', p_query) as q(tsq)
   where c.document_id = any (array(select pr0_proto.toelaatbare_documenten(p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_fonds_id, p_scan, p_lek)))
     and c.zoek_vector @@ q.tsq
     and (p_lek = 'gearchiveerd' or c.documentstatus is distinct from 'gearchiveerd')
   order by rang desc, c.chunk_index asc, c.id
   limit greatest(p_limit, 1)

Query Text: 
  with fts as (
    select f.id, f.document_id, row_number() over (order by f.rang desc, f.id) as r
      from pr0_proto.r1_fts(p_query, p_kandidaten, p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_fonds_id, p_scan, p_lek) f
  ),
  vec as (
    select v.id, v.document_id, row_number() over (order by v.afstand, v.id) as r
      from pr0_proto.r1_vec(p_embedding, p_kandidaten, p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_fonds_id, p_scan, p_lek, p_iteratief, p_strategie) v
  )
  select coalesce(fts.id, vec.id), coalesce(fts.document_id, vec.document_id),
         (coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + fts.r), 0) + coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + vec.r), 0))::real as rang,
         fts.r::int, vec.r::int
    from fts full outer join vec on fts.id = vec.id
   order by 3 desc, 1
   limit p_limit

Query Text: 
  with fts as (
    select f.id, f.document_id, row_number() over (order by f.rang desc, f.id) as r
      from pr0_proto.r1_fts(p_query, p_kandidaten, p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_fonds_id, p_scan, p_lek) f
  ),
  vec as (
    select v.id, v.document_id, row_number() over (order by v.afstand, v.id) as r
      from pr0_proto.r1_vec(p_embedding, p_kandidaten, p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_fonds_id, p_scan, p_lek, p_iteratief, p_strategie) v
  )
  select coalesce(fts.id, vec.id), coalesce(fts.document_id, vec.document_id),
         (coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + fts.r), 0) + coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + vec.r), 0))::real as rang,
         fts.r::int, vec.r::int
    from fts full outer join vec on fts.id = vec.id
   order by 3 desc, 1
   limit p_limit

duration: 8.681 ms  plan:
Query Text: 
  -- Vorm: id-begrensd via `document_id = any(array(...))` ⇒ bitmap-indexscan
  -- op idx_chunks_document (O(zichtbare chunks)); de join-vorm (docs join
  -- chunks) kreeg een seq scan over álle chunks met `@@` als filter. `@@` is
  -- niet leakproof en kan onder RLS nooit via de GIN lopen; hij blijft filter.
  -- `cross join websearch_to_tsquery(...)`: in het generieke plan van een
  -- SQL-functie wordt een inline `websearch_to_tsquery('dutch', $1)` anders per
  -- rij opnieuw geparset (gemeten: 70 ms i.p.v. 9 ms bij gelijke buffers).
  select c.id, c.document_id,
         ts_rank_cd(c.zoek_vector, q.tsq) as rang
    from public.document_chunks c
   cross join websearch_to_tsquery('dutch', p_query) as q(tsq)
   where c.document_id = any (array(select pr0_proto.toelaatbare_documenten(p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_fonds_id, p_scan, p_lek)))
     and c.zoek_vector @@ q.tsq
     and (p_lek = 'gearchiveerd' or c.documentstatus is distinct from 'gearchiveerd')
   order by rang desc, c.chunk_index asc, c.id
   limit greatest(p_limit, 1)

Query Text: 
  with fts as (
    select f.id, f.document_id, row_number() over (order by f.rang desc, f.id) as r
      from pr0_proto.r1_fts(p_query, p_kandidaten, p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_fonds_id, p_scan, p_lek) f
  ),
  vec as (
    select v.id, v.document_id, row_number() over (order by v.afstand, v.id) as r
      from pr0_proto.r1_vec(p_embedding, p_kandidaten, p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_fonds_id, p_scan, p_lek, p_iteratief, p_strategie) v
  )
  select coalesce(fts.id, vec.id), coalesce(fts.document_id, vec.document_id),
         (coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + fts.r), 0) + coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + vec.r), 0))::real as rang,
         fts.r::int, vec.r::int
    from fts full outer join vec on fts.id = vec.id
   order by 3 desc, 1
   limit p_limit

     and (p_lek = 'actueel' or p_modus is distinct from 'actueel'

duration: 8.716 ms  plan:
Query Text: 
  -- Vorm: id-begrensd via `document_id = any(array(...))` ⇒ bitmap-indexscan
  -- op idx_chunks_document (O(zichtbare chunks)); de join-vorm (docs join
  -- chunks) kreeg een seq scan over álle chunks met `@@` als filter. `@@` is
  -- niet leakproof en kan onder RLS nooit via de GIN lopen; hij blijft filter.
  -- `cross join websearch_to_tsquery(...)`: in het generieke plan van een
  -- SQL-functie wordt een inline `websearch_to_tsquery('dutch', $1)` anders per
  -- rij opnieuw geparset (gemeten: 70 ms i.p.v. 9 ms bij gelijke buffers).
  select c.id, c.document_id,
         ts_rank_cd(c.zoek_vector, q.tsq) as rang
    from public.document_chunks c
   cross join websearch_to_tsquery('dutch', p_query) as q(tsq)
   where c.document_id = any (array(select pr0_proto.toelaatbare_documenten(p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_fonds_id, p_scan, p_lek)))
     and c.zoek_vector @@ q.tsq
     and (p_lek = 'gearchiveerd' or c.documentstatus is distinct from 'gearchiveerd')
   order by rang desc, c.chunk_index asc, c.id
   limit greatest(p_limit, 1)

  with fts as (
    select f.id, f.document_id, row_number() over (order by f.rang desc, f.id) as r
      from pr0_proto.r1_fts(p_query, p_kandidaten, p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_fonds_id, p_scan, p_lek) f
  ),
  vec as (
    select v.id, v.document_id, row_number() over (order by v.afstand, v.id) as r
      from pr0_proto.r1_vec(p_embedding, p_kandidaten, p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_fonds_id, p_scan, p_lek, p_iteratief, p_strategie) v
  )
  select coalesce(fts.id, vec.id), coalesce(fts.document_id, vec.document_id),
         (coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + fts.r), 0) + coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + vec.r), 0))::real as rang,
         fts.r::int, vec.r::int
    from fts full outer join vec on fts.id = vec.id
   order by 3 desc, 1
   limit p_limit

      from pr0_proto.r2_fts(p_query, p_kandidaten, p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_scan, p_lek, p_arm) f
  ),
  vec as (
    select v.id, v.document_id, row_number() over (order by v.afstand, v.id) as r
      from pr0_proto.r2_vec(p_embedding, p_kandidaten, p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_scan, p_lek, p_iteratief, p_strategie) v
  )

         (coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + fts.r), 0) + coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + vec.r), 0))::real as rang,
         fts.r::int, vec.r::int
    from fts full outer join vec on fts.id = vec.id
   order by 3 desc, 1
   limit p_limit

    select f.id, f.document_id, row_number() over (order by f.rang desc, f.id) as r
      from pr0_proto.r2_fts(p_query, p_kandidaten, p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_scan, p_lek, p_arm) f
  ),

      from pr0_proto.r2_vec(p_embedding, p_kandidaten, p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_scan, p_lek, p_iteratief, p_strategie) v
  )

         (coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + fts.r), 0) + coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + vec.r), 0))::real as rang,
         fts.r::int, vec.r::int
    from fts full outer join vec on fts.id = vec.id
   order by 3 desc, 1
   limit p_limit

    select f.id, f.document_id, row_number() over (order by f.rang desc, f.id) as r
      from pr0_proto.r2_fts(p_query, p_kandidaten, p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_scan, p_lek, p_arm) f
  ),

         (coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + fts.r), 0) + coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + vec.r), 0))::real as rang,
         fts.r::int, vec.r::int
    from fts full outer join vec on fts.id = vec.id
   order by 3 desc, 1
   limit p_limit

    select f.id, f.document_id, row_number() over (order by f.rang desc, f.id) as r
      from pr0_proto.r2_fts(p_query, p_kandidaten, p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_scan, p_lek, p_arm) f
  ),
  vec as (
    select v.id, v.document_id, row_number() over (order by v.afstand, v.id) as r
      from pr0_proto.r2_vec(p_embedding, p_kandidaten, p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_scan, p_lek, p_iteratief, p_strategie) v
  )
  select coalesce(fts.id, vec.id), coalesce(fts.document_id, vec.document_id),
         (coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + fts.r), 0) + coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + vec.r), 0))::real as rang,
         fts.r::int, vec.r::int
    from fts full outer join vec on fts.id = vec.id
   order by 3 desc, 1
   limit p_limit

    select f.id, f.document_id, row_number() over (order by f.rang desc, f.id) as r
      from pr0_proto.r2_fts(p_query, p_kandidaten, p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_scan, p_lek, p_arm) f
  ),

  )

         (coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + fts.r), 0) + coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + vec.r), 0))::real as rang,
         fts.r::int, vec.r::int
    from fts full outer join vec on fts.id = vec.id
   order by 3 desc, 1
   limit p_limit

    select v.id, v.document_id, row_number() over (order by v.afstand, v.id) as r
      from pr0_proto.r2_vec(p_embedding, p_kandidaten, p_document_ids, p_modus, p_peildatum, p_bronsoort, p_scan, p_lek, p_iteratief, p_strategie) v
  )

         (coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + fts.r), 0) + coalesce(1.0 / (p_k + vec.r), 0))::real as rang,
         fts.r::int, vec.r::int
    from fts full outer join vec on fts.id = vec.id
   order by 3 desc, 1
   limit p_limit
